1. **Identify the black nodes (H and O):**
   - Node H is black and connected to nodes G, C, and I.
   - Node O is black and connected to nodes C, I, and P.

2. **Find all paths between H and O:**
   - Direct path 1: H -> C -> O
   - Direct path 2: H -> I -> O
   - Path 3: H -> G -> E -> L -> M -> F -> X -> K -> J -> P -> O
   - Path 4: H -> G -> B -> N -> C -> O
   - Path 5: H -> G -> B -> N -> Y -> C -> O
   - Path 6: H -> I -> P -> O

3. **Identify critical nodes:**
   - Removing node C severs paths H -> C -> O and paths going through B and N.
   - Removing node I severs paths H -> I -> O and paths through P.
   - Removing node G severs paths going through E, L, M, F, X, K, J, and P.

4. **Determine the minimum cut set:**
   - By analyzing the paths, we see that removing both nodes C and I will disconnect all paths between H and O.
   - This is verified as removing either C or I individually does not disconnect all paths.

### Conclusion:

The minimum number of nodes that must be removed to completely disconnect the two black nodes (H and O) is 2.
